# Junkrat's sound effects: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# cassidy_sfx.txt; his voice is junkrat.txt (junkrat_voice_gen.py). build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them
#  into the local pack.
# Read 2026-11-13 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool / TankLib run):
# his 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded (ww.py, dump_banks.py), the Init bank's game parameter defaults (1E818, stmg.py).
# Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/junkrat/scripts/sounds/ (junkrat_gen.py writes this file from junkrat_table.py; Cassidy's
# pipeline copied and retargeted: fx_graph_junkrat.py, fx_sounds2_junkrat.py, clip_sounds_junkrat.py, contacts.py, ww.py,
# dump_banks.py, extract_banks.py, measure_junkrat.py, overview.py, stmg.py, junkrat_events.py, junkrat_layers.py; new:
# bank_scan_junkrat.py, every STUSound of the build by Wwise bank); their data: research/junkrat/sounds/ (junkrat_ww.json,
# banks_raw.json, junkrat_measure.json, contacts.json, overview.txt, clip_sounds.txt, bank_scan.json) and research/junkrat/work/
# (graph_effects.json, fx_sounds.txt, fx_sounds_timed.json). His skin extraction is research/junkrat/extract and the 43 banks it
# lacked (the frag's slot and contact sounds, the mine's and trap's contacts, the trapped status, the tire's contacts, Nitro
# Boost, the death bomb's burst, the frag bank's unnamed 02Cs) were extracted into research/junkrat/sounds/ExtraSounds/Junkrat
# (extract_banks.py; groups Frag, Mine, Trap, Tire, Perk, Hand, Scan).
# The frag's launch: no effect, clip or graph node of his names one (H: 022B's only effects are behind powers; the 3P add_fire
# clip 2D3E has no animation effect, there is no 1P fire clip; the frag 0385's slots are its hit, burst, flight and bounce).
# junkrat.frag.fire (2D0E, his weapon entity's type-4 contact) and fire_3p (1BFE, an unnamed 02C of the frag's bank) are L:
# replace them if a later read finds the launch's effect.
# Not mapped: 0186 / 0FCB (the shared ability-denied, as Cassidy's); the perks' and powers' effects but Frag Cannon (022B #462)
# and Nitro Boost (51E8 #409 / #410): 022B #478 6BA4 D83C (2625.134), #493 6BA8 D840 / D923 (2626.134), 096A #645 / #649 /
# #666 / #668 625C 8171 and 65F6 C424 (a perk's mine), 096A #639 / 09BA #587 5F58 BB32 (a game-message switch, L which perk),
# 096B #506 / #510 64C5 / 659C C3BC (252E.134), 022A #241 6C6F (2623.134), 0233 / 0234 3827 (014C.134), 51E8 #442 6740 (no
# sound); 022A #45 19A0 (3777 has no files); 0234 #255 4557 (11F9 / 11FA, 32-56 dB down, on its StateAnim alias A0, L what);
# the mine's m_306ABA15 slot 2A0E (2C4A / 2C49, L when); the trap entity's 151A (09BB #827, the mine's blast, L when) and its
# child 6707's 6705 C79A (a 30 m bed, L when) and 16D5 (a ribbon, no sound); 09BC #103 19F4 387F / 5DDC (a second status
# look, L which); 039C's contact 2A71 (no files); the frag bank's other unnamed 02Cs ED86 / ED87 / B176 (explosion variants),
# EDA8 / B177 / EE0F (L what); the frag's 0245 / 00FA other materials; 8BC5 (the shared character hit); the contact type 4
# of the mine-in-hand 04E8 (055B 3637, 150 dB down); the clips' body foley (2CBE / 2CDF / 2CBF / 2CDC / 2CBB / 2CDD / 2CBC /
# 2CDE) and the emote / highlight / select clips (2D7x, 32Bx, 3B5F..., 93Bx, 98Bx); a wall-climb or tire-climb sound (0234
# names none); a Steel Trap or RIP-Tire cast sound of 0232 / 09BA themselves (they name only 0186); 004B / 0033 / 01C7
# shared effects (ult charge, health, regen); footsteps (as Cassidy's).
# Ids: 02C sounds (5DC5 = 000000005DC5.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs
# 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess. Stadium powers' sets are left out.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in sojourn.txt, ONE offset per family keeps
# his sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter catches
# sums): own -6.5 dB (loudest: junkrat.tire.explode), 3p -3 dB (loudest: junkrat.mine.hit_enemy_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: audience-1 copies and no-range copies are the player the effect plays on ('own': Junkrat, the shooter for
# a hit, the victim for the trap's hold, M as in Sigma's map); ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p'); where two
#  ranged copies hold the same files the
# louder is built (L which team's / near or far). A sound with no own copy is built once for everyone ('both').

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# junkrat.frag.fire: Frag Launcher shot, own: 2D0E (a random 7 of 0.30-0.77 s low thumps at +752 cents). L: see the trigger.
# Trigger: PRIMARY (022B #98 -> #136 StateWeaponVolley, the frag 0385, H). No effect or clip names a launch sound (H);
#  this 02C is read from the data around it, not from a node: the Frag Launcher entity 0452's contact set 00F9, type
#  4, material 0001 -> 1733 -> 2D0E (30 m, audience 2), the only sound his weapon entity names; type 4 read as the
#  weapon's own contact (L), its files the same low thumps (partials 57 / 68 / 80 / 104 / 113 Hz) as 1BFE's (M).
# Layers: a 2D0E 649347409 RanSeq 0.30-0.77 s x7 +752 cents, net -25.
@junkrat.frag.fire = junkrat.frag.fire.a*1.544:-31.5

# LOOP LENGTH 0.583 s: play it again every LOOP LENGTH (the built file runs 0.58 s: shorter layers are tiled to fill
# the loop and the last tile runs over; Overwatch's layers loop on their own lengths, which one file cannot do).

# ===== For the integration: each event, loop or one-shot, who it is built for, its length, and what triggers it
# (own = the player the effect plays on, in the head: Junkrat, the shooter, or the trapped victim; 3p = everyone else, at him
# / the frag / the mine / the trap / the tire; both = one rule for everyone). 'EXTRA' = in the data but not needed for the
# kit's moments. Not built: see 'Not mapped'.
#   junkrat.frag.fire                  one-shot own  0.50 s; PRIMARY (022B #98 -> #136 StateWeaponVolley, the frag
#     0385, H). No effect or clip names a launch sound (H); this 02C is read from the data around it, not from a node:
#     the Frag Launcher entity 0452's contact set 00F9, type 4, material 0001 -> 1733 -> 2D0E (30 m, audience 2), the
#     only sound his weapon entity names; type 4 read as the weapon's own contact (L), its files the same low thumps
#     (partials 57 / 68 / 80 / 104 / 113 Hz) as 1BFE's (M)
#   junkrat.frag.fire_3p               one-shot 3p   2.86 s; PRIMARY (022B #98 -> #136 StateWeaponVolley, the frag
#     0385, H). No effect or clip names a launch sound (H); this 02C is read from the data around it, not from a node:
#     1BFE (150 m, audience 2) is a STUSound of the frag's Wwise bank 1E673 that no effect we read names
#     (bank_scan_junkrat.py: every 02C of the build by bank); its 0.6-1.3 s thumps and 1.8-2.7 s tails read as the
#     launch heard by others (L)
#   junkrat.frag.flight                LOOP     own  loop 0.583 s; each frag in flight: its child entity 03C9's
#     STUEffectComponent 051B -> 2B52 (20 m, audience 1: his, H ids, M who), held while the frag lives
#   junkrat.frag.flight_3p             LOOP     3p   loop 1.615 s; the same frag for everyone else: 051B's 2B51 (20 m,
#     audience 2: 2B52's files) and 2B4E (8 m, a 2.0 s rattle), held (H ids, M who)

#     is the same pair of containers)
